cholesky/cholesky.cpp:11:// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:12:// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:13:
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:14:bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:15:    // A is stored in row-major order
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:16:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:17:        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:18:            double sum = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:19:            
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:20:            if (i == j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:21:                // Diagonal element
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:22:                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:23:                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:24:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:25:                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:26:                if (val <= 0.0) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:27:                    // Matrix is not positive definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:28:                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:29:                    return false;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:30:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:31:                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:32:            } else {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:33:                // Off-diagonal element
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:34:                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:35:                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:36:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:37:                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:38:            }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:39:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:40:        
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:41:        // Zero out upper triangular part
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:42:        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:43:            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:44:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:45:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:46:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:47:    return true;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:48:}

cholesky/cholesky.cpp:50:// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:51:void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:52:    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:53:    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:54:    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:55:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:56:    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:57:    unsigned int seed = 42;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:58:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:59:    // Generate random matrix B
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:60:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:61:        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:62:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:63:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:64:    // Compute A = B * B^T
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:65:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:66:        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:67:            double sum = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:68:            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:69:                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:70:            }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:71:            A[i * n + j] = sum;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:72:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:73:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:74:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:75:    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:76:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:77:        A[i * n + i] += n;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:78:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:79:}

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cholesky_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(cholesky cholesky.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cholesky PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(cholesky m)

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Each MPI rank owns a contiguous set of rows. At step k, the owner of row k
+// broadcasts the panel row; every GPU updates its local rows independently.
+__global__ void update_rows(double* a, const double* panel, size_t n,
+                            size_t first, size_t count, size_t k) {
+    const size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (q >= count * n) return;
+    const size_t r = q / n, j = q % n, i = first + r;
+    if (i <= k || j <= k || j >= n) return;
+    const double lik = a[r * n + k] / panel[k];
+    // Column normalization is shared by all elements in this row. Store once
+    // from the first trailing-column thread.
+    if (j == k + 1) a[r * n + k] = lik;
+    a[r * n + j] -= lik * panel[j];
+}

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L,const std::vector<double>& A,size_t n) {
+    double maxError=0, relError=0;
+    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError,relError) schedule(static)
+    for(long long ij=0;ij<static_cast<long long>(n*n);++ij) {
+        size_t i=ij/n,j=ij%n; double sum=0;
+        for(size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum+=L[i*n+k]*L[j*n+k];
+        double e=fabs(sum-A[ij]); maxError=std::max(maxError,e);
+        relError=std::max(relError,e/(fabs(A[ij])+1e-10));
+    }
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\nMax relative error: %.10e\n",maxError,relError);
+    return relError<=1e-6;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Each MPI rank owns a contiguous set of rows. At step k, the owner of row k
+// broadcasts the panel row; every GPU updates its local rows independently.
+__global__ void update_rows(double* a, const double* panel, size_t n,
+                            size_t first, size_t count, size_t k) {
+    const size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (q >= count * n) return;
+    const size_t r = q / n, j = q % n, i = first + r;
+    if (i <= k || j <= k || j >= n) return;
+    const double lik = a[r * n + k] / panel[k];
+    // Column normalization is shared by all elements in this row. Store once
+    // from the first trailing-column thread.
+    if (j == k + 1) a[r * n + k] = lik;
+    a[r * n + j] -= lik * panel[j];
+}

+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L,const std::vector<double>& A,size_t n) {
+    double maxError=0, relError=0;

void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, size_t n) {
    std::vector<double> B(n*n);
    unsigned int seed=42;
    for(size_t i=0;i<n*n;++i) B[i]=(rand_r(&seed)/(double)RAND_MAX)-0.5;
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for(long long ij=0;ij<static_cast<long long>(n*n);++ij) {
        size_t i=ij/n,j=ij%n; double sum=0;
        for(size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum+=B[i*n+k]*B[j*n+k];
        A[ij]=sum;
    }
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for(long long i=0;i<static_cast<long long>(n);++i) A[i*n+i]+=n;
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L,const std::vector<double>& A,size_t n) {
    double maxError=0, relError=0;
    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError,relError) schedule(static)
    for(long long ij=0;ij<static_cast<long long>(n*n);++ij) {
        size_t i=ij/n,j=ij%n; double sum=0;
        for(size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum+=L[i*n+k]*L[j*n+k];
        double e=fabs(sum-A[ij]); maxError=std::max(maxError,e);
        relError=std::max(relError,e/(fabs(A[ij])+1e-10));
    }
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\nMax relative error: %.10e\n",maxError,relError);
    return relError<=1e-6;
}

codex
I implemented MPI row partitioning, CUDA row updates, OpenMP matrix generation and validation, and updated the CMake configuration while keeping the executable name `cholesky`.

The code builds, but validation fails: for `-n 64 -v`, the single-rank run reports a maximum relative error of about `6.7e-3` (the required limit is `1e-6`). I wasn’t able to complete the correctness fix, so this implementation is not ready to use. The multi-rank validation run did not complete after the single-rank failure.

+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L,const std::vector<double>& A,size_t n) {
+    double maxError=0, relError=0;
+    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError,relError) schedule(static)
+    for(long long ij=0;ij<static_cast<long long>(n*n);++ij) {
+        size_t i=ij/n,j=ij%n; double sum=0;
+        for(size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum+=L[i*n+k]*L[j*n+k];
+        double e=fabs(sum-A[ij]); maxError=std::max(maxError,e);
+        relError=std::max(relError,e/(fabs(A[ij])+1e-10));
+    }
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\nMax relative error: %.10e\n",maxError,relError);
+    return relError<=1e-6;
 }

tokens used
40,319
I implemented MPI row partitioning, CUDA row updates, OpenMP matrix generation and validation, and updated the CMake configuration while keeping the executable name `cholesky`.

The code builds, but validation fails: for `-n 64 -v`, the single-rank run reports a maximum relative error of about `6.7e-3` (the required limit is `1e-6`). I wasn’t able to complete the correctness fix, so this implementation is not ready to use. The multi-rank validation run did not complete after the single-rank failure.
